## Source disagreements

Each item gives both sources, the numbers, and which one is trusted and why.

**Production meters and indexes against the chain**

- **Settlement meter against the chain.** `/api/stats/public` shows 2 settlements and 0.30 USDC. The chain shows 20 settlements and 3.30 USDC since the meter's commit e090bd42 (2026-07-05T16:33Z). **Trust the chain**: it is a full RPC log scan with 0 failed chunks. The meter's 2 rows are exactly the two self settlements into 0x0295.
- **`/api/stats` revenueEst against the chain.** `/api/stats` shows $5.51 (runs x price, 99 runs); the chain shows 2 paid settlements (0.30 USDC) to the current payee. **Trust the chain**: the run counter also counts free MCP bypass calls (`mcp/route.ts:286`), and at least 1 of the 99 is the audit's own `blue_doctor` call.
- **Blockscout against RPC on 0x0295.** Blockscout leaves out the 0.025 ACP inflow, returns 404 for the completion tx, and shows all-zero counters. The `balanceOf` series proves the inflow. **Trust RPC.**
- **ACP gross against net.** `/api/acp/revenue` shows `usdc_gross` 0.5; the seller wallet's only inflow is 0.45 (0.025 went to Virtuals and 0.025 back to 0x0295). Both are correct as labelled.
- **Hub community counter against the chain.** `/api/hub/tools` shows revenueTotal 20000 (0.02 USDC via the Hub); its own route header calls this an estimate. The chain shows 4 payments to the builder, and it cannot tell which ones went through the Hub. **Trust the chain for amounts.** The split is undetermined.
- **x402scan against RPC.** No disagreement: 0xb058 at 145 tx, 36.16 USDC, 11 buyers; 0x0295 at 2 tx, 0.30 USDC, 2 buyers.

**CLAUDE.md claims against measurement**

- **Bankr in the payment path.** CLAUDE.md says there is 'no Bankr in the payment path'. The Bankr storefront quotes 402 for 30 ids and settled payments through 2026-09-19, reached by published packages. **Trust the measurement.** The claim holds for blueagent.dev only.
- **Bankr callers and key readers.** CLAUDE.md says nothing in the repo calls Bankr and the key has zero readers. There are 3 readers in the committed `services/x402-server` (`llm.ts:10`, `index.ts:29`, `routes/ecosystem-digest.ts:103`). **Trust `git grep`.** That directory is a whole second x402 backend with a launchd plist, not a stray file (section 1c).
- **hub_ names map to catalog ids.** CLAUDE.md says 'Every `hub_` name there maps to a real catalog id, so that surface is clean', which is 10 of 10. Measured: 9 of the 10 `hub_` names map through `HUB_MAP` (`mcp/route.ts:124-146`). `hub_hood_arrow` is an inline branch commented 'not a catalog tool' (`mcp/route.ts:961-962`), which reads `/api/hood/arrows` (:731). **Trust the code.** No `hub_` name points at a dead id, so 'clean' holds. The mapping claim does not.
- **Who writes Aeon KV.** CLAUDE.md says the `aeon:<skill>` keys are 'written by `/api/cron/research-loop`, read by 15 paid x402 handlers'. Measured: research-loop writes only `aeon:deep-research`, which 5 handlers read. 9 handlers read `aeon:narrative-tracker` or `aeon:token-movers`, whose only writer is `/api/aeon-feed`. 1 reads `aeon:digest`, which has no writer. **Trust the code** (`git grep setAeonOutput HEAD`).
- **Venice key.** CLAUDE.md calls `VENICE_INFERENCE_KEY` a dead env var. It has 4 runtime readers (`llm.ts:649,730`; `chat/route.ts:3102`; `crypto-rpc/route.ts:77`). **Trust the code.** The user's memory note already says Venice is live.
- **Quantum tools.** CLAUDE.md says there are 'no quantum tools anywhere'. quantum-* ids are defined in at least 8 lists across 23 files. None is a live catalog id. **Trust the parse**: the claim holds only for the live catalog and MCP.
- **Manifest size.** CLAUDE.md says '20,536 bytes'. The figure is 20,536 UTF-16 code units, which is 20,736 UTF-8 bytes (the full response body is 20,803 bytes). The count agrees; the unit is wrong.
- **Shim importers.** CLAUDE.md says the shims have '~46 legacy importers', and the `llm.ts` header says 20 paid handlers load the MiroShark prompt. The measurements: 2 real importers of the shim, and 0 handlers reach the injection. **Trust the trace.**
- **CLAUDE.md numbers that agree with measurement:** catalog 114; MCP 19 (8 + 10 + 1); skill 49 (15 `blue_` + 34 `hub_`); skill and MCP overlap by 6; `PAYMENT_WALLET` has 0 readers.

**Stale numbers in code comments**

- `_handlers/index.ts:10` says '115 == 115 today'; the import measures 114 == 114.
- `mcp-tools.ts:105` says 'DO NOT ADD A 19th'; the array already holds 19.
- The poll header says '60s scheduler (GitHub Actions)'; it is Vercel Cron */5.
- The brief-worker header says 'every 1 min'; it runs */3.
- The sparkline header recommends 15 min; it runs */30.
- `kv-keys.ts:94` says the Base archive has 'no READ route yet'; `/api/hood/base-series` serves it.
- Commit eac65d23 called */30 with a 20-min TTL 'safe'; 0 of 24 sparklines were measured.
- `credit-pricing.ts:127-129` advertises 20% and 40% discounts; the discount is always 0.
- `spend-summary.ts:45` says 'ref is unset on every chat debit'; chat debits have carried refs since 7fe7cf53.
- A comment in the chat route says `git log -S` for dca-executor is empty on every branch. Side-branch commit 5788f443 added it to the root `vercel.json`, which Vercel ignores. The first half of the comment, that it was never in `apps/web/vercel.json`, is true.

**Code behaviour against claims made to users**

- **blue_doctor known issues.** It says 'wallet-holdings, wallet-risk and the onchain-activity tools return 502 by design'. 2 of the 9 Moralis tools return 502; 7 return 200 (confirmed in code and by local runs). **Trust the code.** The memory note saying these tools 502 has the same error.
- **`/api/wallet/transactions`.** Its header says it 'degrades to an empty list + needsKey flag when absent'. Measured: HTTP 200 with body error 'moralis 401'. **Trust the measurement.**
- **Credits for tool calls.** `/docs/credits` says 'you never spend chat credits on a tool call'. `x402/[tool]/route.ts` L315 debits credits, and the deployer's ledger shows `tool:token-pick-signal` 400. `/app/plans` also contradicts `/docs/credits`. **Trust the code and the ledger.**
- **How top-ups are paid.** `/app/plans` and `lib/credits.ts:10` say top-ups are 'Coinbase x402'. They are a plain transfer (`payments.ts:18-24`), and the verifier found the route accepts any Transfer log. **Trust the code.**
- **Credit packs.** `ChatInput.tsx:448` says 'USDC credit packs coming soon'. The purchase flow has been live since 956af0c8 (2026-08-06), one day before the string was added. **Trust the code and git.**
- **Scheduled results.** `CronPanel.tsx:341` says results are posted 'into Blue Chat'. No code path does that. **Trust the code.**
- **`/api/stats` access.** The header says 'Gated by ?key='. The route is public (`route.ts:5` against `:60`).

**Published packages and docs against the catalog**

- **agentkit.** Repo source (1.3.0) has 12 tools, all live. npm latest (0.1.0) has 32, of which 20 are dead. **Trust npm for what users install.**
- **Prices.** The catalog is what the route charges, so it is trusted over all of these:
  - The CLI menu shows the wrong price for all 12 live ids; grant-evaluator shows $0.01 against $5.00.
  - vercel-ai and langchain show the wrong price for 7 of 12.
  - `apps/docs/api/x402.mdx` has 7 dead ids, plus risk-gate at $0.10 against $0.20 and grant-evaluator at $0.50 against $5.00.
  - The x402-client comments show 2 wrong prices.
- **Tool counts in package docs.**
  - The claude-plugin marketplace says '15 Hub tools'. `/api/mcp` serves 10 `hub_` tools, and the plugin itself has 14 invocable skills plus 1 retired pointer.
  - `apps/docs/quickstart.mdx:63` says the CLI has '18 commands + 31 x402 tools'. Measured: 24 other items and 30 x402 entries (29 unique).
  - The x402-client README says '40 AI tools'.
- **blue_registry.** Its totals advertise 120 ids. A single response lists at most 60, and only 114 can be run.

**Within the audit: measurer against verifier.** In each case the verifier is trusted, for the reason given.

- **2b upstream counts.** LLM tools 65 against 64; Etherscan 14 against 13; RH RPC '11 or more' against '14 or more'; safe-trending's class. The verifier used a function-level trace, and the handler source itself says 'NO LLM anywhere'.
- **3, weekly first fire.** 'Today or tomorrow' against 'today or 7 days out'. The verifier ran HEAD `nextFireAt`.
- **4, the purchase route.**
  - 'Checks transfer()' against 'sums any Transfer log'.
  - 'At most one purchase' against '0 to 4 purchase:usdc events possible'.
  - The verifier read the route source and the receipts.
- **7, payer attribution.**
  - Storefront payments after the apps/api deletion: 2 against 1, from the deletion commit date compared with the payment timestamp. The fixer pass shows that the cut-off itself does not apply, because the storefront was never apps/api (section 6).
  - 0xce17a154: self against insider.
  - 0x8402d32e: '7702 Bankr-type' against a plain EOA.
  - B20HUB: 0 swaps against 30 swaps in legacy pools.
- **1a summary against group counts.** The summary says '27 live and in a nav'; the class has 29 rows. Not a disagreement: 2 of those rows are nav-check rows, not pages.
- **1a sitemap against section 6.** 1a measured 2 of the 115 redirects and inferred 113 from the middleware rule. Section 6 measured all 114 `/app/hub/<id>` (each 301, every target 200). The result agrees, and the inference is now a measurement.
- **The fixer brief against measurement.** Three premises in the brief for the fixer pass did not survive:
  - 'aeon.yml exports BANKR_LLM_KEY as ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN and posts to Telegram, Discord, Slack and SendGrid'. The export sits behind `gateway.provider == bankr`, and the config says `direct`. Slack and SendGrid have no secrets. **Trust the config and the secret list.**
  - 'farcaster.json is shadowed by public/'. It is the other way round: production serves the route's compact JSON. **Trust the bytes.**
  - 'the 8 console runs are MCP-console usage'. `blue_idea` is not in `CONSOLE_MAP`, so at least 4 came from another `/api/console` caller. **Trust the map.**
  - The brief's run counts match: 36 scheduled runs and 50 Aeon runs, except that 1 of the 50 failed.
- **5, the Vercel MCP.** A memory note says the Vercel MCP returns 403 for everything. Measured: `list_projects` works, while `get_project` and runtime logs return 403. The failure is partial and depends on scope.

**Questions one pass left open that another pass answered**

- **Is the prod openapi fresh?** 2a saw a STALE copy (age 5143 s). Its verifier re-fetched and got a HIT (age 651 s) with the same 114 paths.
- **What do the published agentkit 0.1.0 and core 1.0.4 contain?** 2a and 1b did not read the tarballs. Section 6 did: agentkit has 32 ids (20 dead) and posts to `/api/tools`, which returns 404; core's tool-inputs has 32 keys (20 dead).
- **Who controls 0x64d1c1, the 0.2 USDC x402 payer?** Section 4 left this open. Section 7 found it is self: a nonce-0 wallet funded by 0xD5C1 51 minutes before it paid.
- **Is the Bankr storefront still live?** Section 7 left this open. Section 6 probed it: it is live, quoting 402 for 30 of 31 ids.
- **Did legacy B20HUB deployments earn fees?** Section 7's measurer left this open. Skeptic B found 30 swaps and one self-generated fee.
- **Did any non-owner buy credits on-chain?** Section 4 could not rule it out from balance checkpoints. Section 7's full log scan shows only 4 USDC inflows into 0x0295 ever, all self. So on-chain, no non-owner purchase happened since 2026-03-01. How many `purchase:usdc` ledger events exist still needs KV.

## What surprised us

- ACP is entirely self-dealing: the x402 treasury was both buyer and evaluator on all 5 jobs.
- The Bankr storefront, which the repo records as removed, is still quoting 30 ids (18 of them not in the catalog) and settled a payment on 2026-09-19. The official installer still sends new users there, and to the banned LLM host.
- B20HUB has returned 404 since the day it was created, while a CI guard counts it as the surviving replacement for the Bankr launch path.
- 7 of the 9 Moralis-backed paid tools keep charging during the outage. They issue verdict words from absent data: PASS, NEUTRAL, CAUTION, INACTIVE, and a 'Newcomer' score of 0.
- The page the brief named, `/app/scheduled`, does not exist. The feature is `/app/cron`.
- The credit purchase route credits any USDC Transfer into the treasury, x402 settlements included. Free airdrop credits (29,000) outnumber all purchased credits (at most 10,000) in the same `topup` field.
- OPTIONS returns 204 for any path on blueagent.dev, so a liveness probe based on OPTIONS would mark every retired route as alive.
- The public settlement meter under-reports the chain by 18 settlements, and what it does show is entirely self traffic.
- External-looking payers clustered in late May and June 2026, just after the x402 and /.well-known listings went up. None has paid a blueagent.dev payee since 2026-07-05.
- The paid tools `rh-stock-alert` and `rh-rwa-dca` sell an 'armed' alert and a recurring schedule that no code ever reads.
- An internal contract-deploy page, `/app/robinhood-router`, is publicly reachable.
- A BlueAgent-named job still runs every day outside the repo. `madebyshun/blueagent-aeon` made 36 scheduled runs and 50 skill runs in the last 7 days. It posts to Telegram, and its memory file names t.me/blueagent_hub. It also posts to a Discord-webhook secret created the same day as `/api/aeon-feed`. Meanwhile the repo records Aeon as retired and treats its KV as expired.
- The live Bankr storefront is not the deleted apps/api. It is deployed from `blueagent-x402-services`, whose deploy workflow is still active, and every one of its 31 handlers calls the banned `llm.bankr.bot`. Its risk-gate answers 200 BLOCK when that call fails.
- A tenth published package, `@blueagent/x402-guard`, has no source in any repo checked. It signs x402 payments with a caller-supplied private key and pays the Bankr storefront, under a README that links to blueagent.dev.
- The `/hub` OG image file is not served today: its URL lands on a 'Tool not found' page on the app host.

## What we could not determine, and why

- **(7) Whether any of the 13 unrefuted payers is truly external**, and whether cluster A or any other payer belongs to a wallet ShunTr controls outside the 9 known ones. The chain cannot prove ownership. The scans exclude native ETH (public RPC has no trace API), activity before 2026-03-01 (Blockscout returned 429 on self histories) and self wallets nobody has identified. Cluster A dates to 2023; the oldest self wallet found dates to 2025-01-09.
- **(7) Which tools the unattributed payers bought, and through which client** (Hub UI or `@blueagent/x402`). Per-payer receipts sit in private KV `spend:<payer>` with a 90-day TTL. `/api/usage/daily` returned 401 without CRON_SECRET. Amounts do not map uniquely to tools, and both clients produce the same signature.
- **(7) Request-level caller evidence** (IPs, user agents, MCP clients). Vercel runtime logs returned 403 on both aggregate and line queries. The vercel CLI was not tried as a workaround.
- **(7) Which 2 of the 4 payments to the community builder went through the Blue Hub.** All 4 were settled by the builder's facilitator relayer. The Hub counter records no payer, and its revenue figure is an estimate.
- **(7) How many of the 23 wallets that spent free credits are external.** `/api/stats/public` publishes counts only, and ledger rows are per-wallet KV.
- **(7) Who funded 0xee79f176 on Ethereum L1** (behind 0x01d80ecf). That needs an archive or trace L1 source, and only a non-archive node was available.
- **(7) Whether 0x00f04a2a's signature pattern** (validAfter 0, validBefore now+3600) **comes from a Bankr platform path or a private client.** Bankr's client code is not in the repo, and no self payment used that pattern.
- **(7) Who owns exchange hot wallet 0x3304e22d.** It is shared by a self wallet's first funding, by 0xf7d1b9f8 and by 0x81b73786's funder. Blockscout has no tag for it, and with a nonce of 6.5M, sharing it is not evidence of common ownership.
- **(7) Storefront payments that Bankr accepted but never forwarded to 0xf31f59.** Buyers pay Bankr's shared router, the resource path is not on-chain, and x402scan does not index Bankr's facilitator.
- **(7) Which package, if any, the unattributed storefront payers used.** Nothing on-chain records the client, and Bankr's logs are not accessible.
- **(7) Whether the 11 sniper-pattern swappers in legacy B20HUB pools are external, and whether they count as users.** Their funding was not traced. They called Uniswap V4 through routers, never a BlueAgent endpoint, and their LP fees are stuck in the broken hook.
- **(7) Stakers in BlueMarketStaking 0x69e5...279d.** It is not a paid surface (retired, 0 USDC). Enumerating stakers needs a scan of about 5M blocks of logs, which was not run.
- **(7) Inflows before 2026-03-01, and non-USDC or native-ETH payments to the payees.** Scans start at block 42,766,926, and native ETH leaves no logs.
- **(7) DCA keeper approvals or fees.** Keeper addresses derive from KEEPER_MASTER_KEY, which is secret. The executor never ran.
- **(7) A cross-check of the on-chain numbers against a second RPC.** publicnode returned 403 partway through and Blockscout returned 429. Every number comes from `mainnet.base.org`, with 0 failed chunks.
- **(1) What a real `/share/[id]` renders.** Routing was measured with a dummy id (200, 'not found / expired' state). Real share ids exist only in KV and are created by a POST, which is a write, and there is no public listing.
- **(1) What a real registry profile renders.** Routing was measured with a dummy handle: the root twin 301s and the app route answers 200. The view is a client component, and no real handle exists (the registry total is 0).
- **(1) Whether the pages that return 200 actually work in a browser.** curl runs no JavaScript, and most app pages are client components behind wallet connect or client-side fetches. Only `/agent/[handle]` was confirmed broken. `/builder/[handle]` depends on the LLM-backed `/api/builder-score`, which was not called.
- **(1) `/app/hub/builders/[address]` for a real builder.** No builder address is known. The probe used the payTo address, and the server renders any valid address, so the result proves only that the route resolves.
- **(1) Whether orphan pages are truly unreachable.** The inbound scan covered `apps/web/src`, `public/` and `next.config.ts` only. It skipped X and Telegram posts, packages, the README, the Mintlify docs and third-party sites, and no traffic data was read.
- **(1) An exact inbound link count for `/`.** Regex matching over-counts, so a curated nav list was used instead.
- **(1) Whether `/pledge`, `/claim`, `/about` and `/soul` still serve their purpose.** That is a product question the code cannot answer. The pledge page has closed-state logic, but which state production shows was not checked.
- **(1) How `docs.blueagent.dev` is handled, and whether `apps/docs` (the Mintlify source whose x402 table has 7 dead ids) is published, and where.** middleware.ts has a 301-to-Mintlify branch, but it was not probed. No hostname appears in the files read.
- **(1) What POST, PUT and DELETE handlers do in production.** No POSTs were allowed, so every write-path side effect comes from reading HEAD.
- **(1) GET status for builder-score, health, status, hub/tools/health, hood/ticker-detail and auth/nonce.** Each GET has a side effect: an LLM call, a Virtuals probe, third-party requests, a cache fill, or a nonce written to KV. Their existence is confirmed by the Allow header only.
- **(1) Whether any external party calls the 38 zero-caller routes** (acp/*, hood/dislocation, bazaar, v1/healthz, signals and others). No request logs were read.
- **(1) How complete the caller counts are.** They come from static text matching. 3 misses were found and fixed by hand, and URLs built at runtime may still be undercounted.
- **(1) Whether `services/x402-server` runs anywhere, and whether its tunnel answers.** It is not running on the audit host. The README's Mac Mini could not be reached from the audit, and `TUNNEL_BASE_URL`'s value (the likely tunnel URL) was not read.
- **(1) Whether the `/hub` OG image was ever served.** It is not served today. Earlier middleware states were not replayed.
- **(1) Whether agent-registry/submit's Aeon calls still reach the LLM.** That depends on a GitHub SKILL.md loading at runtime. It was not fetched, and the route was not called.
- **(1) Whether a GET of hood/arrows rewrote the derived KV feed cache.** This was not recorded per call; it is a disposable cache.
- **(2) The current Virtuals credit balance, and each LLM tool's own code path.** The production key answered at 03:24:47Z (brief-worker) and ran out of credits twice in 7 days. How much credit is left, and whether any given tool's call succeeds, needs the balance or a paid call. No LLM-backed tool was called. This still decides whether token-alpha, whale-tracker, airdrop-check and aml-screen ever reach their LLM branch.
- **(2) Whether Etherscan v2 works with our key, and whether its account and token modules work on 8453 on our plan.** Keyed. The in-repo claim that the account module is 'unsupported on the free tier' (moralis.ts header, 2026-09-26) was not re-measured.
- **(2) Whether Vercel's egress also gets RH Blockscout's Cloudflare 403**, which would break rh-stock-holders, rh-stock-new-listings, rh-stock-pnl and rh-rwa-verify provenance. It was observed from the audit host only. A probe through blue_call on the $0.00 rh-rwa-verify was denied by the permission classifier and not pursued another way.
- **(2) Whether `aeon:narrative-tracker` and `aeon:token-movers` are live in production**, which decides what 9 paid tools serve. The off-repo Aeon job runs both skills daily and posts to a `DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL` secret created 5.5 h after `/api/aeon-feed` was committed with instructions to point that secret at it. But the secret's value was not read, the job sends the response to `/dev/null`, and reading KV needs credentials. `aeon:deep-research` and `aeon:digest` have no running writer, so those 6 readers take the null path. Section 5 shows research-loop has not run for at least 14 days.
- **(2) What the KV-backed tools read from KV** (hood-live, hood-track-record, blue-registry, narrative-scan, rh-rwa-dca, rh-stock-alert). KV itself answered in production (`/api/usage`, `/api/stats`, `/api/hood/health`). The keys each tool reads were not read. Keyed; blue_doctor also reports KV as 'unknown'.
- **(2) Whether `BASE_RPC_URL` overrides `mainnet.base.org` in production.** Env values were not read.
- **(2) How fresh the Chainlink feeds on the RH RPC are.** The RPC is live, but no feed's updatedAt was read.
- **(2) Exact upstreams, function by function, for every id beyond the classes verified.** The measurer used hand-mapped function tables. Only safe-trending and the 3 ids with the cross-chain hint were shown to be wrong; other ids may carry smaller errors.
- **(2) Whether any `rh-alert:*` or `rh-dca:*` records exist in production**, which would mean callers paid for an 'armed' alert or a schedule that never fires. KV credentials are needed.
- **(2) What blue_registry actually returns in production.** It was not called, to keep a metered call out of the counters. The 120 comes from the code plus `/api/hub/tools`.
- **(2) Whether the 6 community tool endpoints answer.** They are third-party tunnels, and were not probed.
- **(2) Which ids are registered on the ERC-8257 ToolRegistry 0x265BB2DBFC0A8165C9A1941Eb1372F349baD2cf1 (Base 8453).** The contract has code, but its ABI is unknown and Blockscout returned an internal error. No function selectors were guessed. The script's '13 already registered' is a comment, not evidence.
- **(2) Whether the langchain package is on PyPI under another name.** Only `blueagent-langchain` was checked (404).
- **(2) Which list "the schema list" in the brief meant.** It is ambiguous, so both readings are answered.
- **(2) The contents of published npm tarballs that no pass read.** Section 6 read the tarballs it cites. Everything else about published builds comes from `npm view` metadata.
- **(3) Whether `/api/cron/user-tasks` fired in the last 7 days.** Vercel runtime logs returned 403. Its only traces (`crons:next`, `crons:owners`, `crons:w:*`) are in KV, and the unauthenticated GET returns 401 before reading anything.
- **(3) How many wallets are enrolled and how many tasks exist.** These are in the Redis SET `crons:owners` and in `crons:w:<wallet>`, which need KV credentials. `/api/chat/schedule` returns only the caller's own wallet, behind SIWE. Foreground tasks live only in each browser's localStorage.
- **(3) Whether any scheduled run has charged a real user.** Ledger entries are not tagged as scheduled, and reading the ledger needs secrets.
- **(3) Whether CRON_SECRET is set in production**, which the background path needs. Env values were not read, and a 401 looks the same either way.
- **(3) Whether the 8-runs-per-tick double-charge risk has ever triggered** against the 300 s limit. That needs runtime logs or KV history.
- **(3) Whether `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` points the executor's self-fetch at blueagent.dev.** It is an env value. The code falls back to `https://blueagent.dev`.
- **(3) Whether the foreground auto-run reads a stale crons list when walletReady and walletAddr settle in the same render.** That needs a browser with a wallet.
- **(3) The effective maxDuration of `/api/cron/run`.** The route exports none, so it depends on the project's Fluid compute setting, which is not visible from the repo.
- **(3, 1, 5) Whether Vercel's edge strips an `x-vercel-cron` header sent by a client.** This decides whether the dca-executor and b20hub-distribute bypass can be exploited. Testing it would run code that moves funds.
- **(4) How many `purchase:usdc` credit events exist (0 to 4 possible), and whether anyone redeemed an x402 settlement or the ACP inbound as credits.** That needs the KV `purchase:<txHash>` markers. POSTing to the purchase route is forbidden.
- **(4) Whether the deployer's topup of 10000 is exactly the 2026-08-08 purchase.** The balance route returns only the last 10 events, and all 10 are spends.
- **(4) Whether `/api/credits/topup` (the admin mint) was ever used, and how much it minted.** It is gated by the internal key, has no callers in the repo, and no aggregate splits `topup` by reason.
- **(4) How much of the 23 wallets, 12,590 credits and 117 messages is self traffic.** It is an aggregate over capped history with no addresses exposed.
- **(4) How often metering failed open in production.** That needs runtime logs.
- **(4) Whether INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY is set in production.** It is a secret. Indirect evidence says yes: chat debits in the deployer's ledger carry refs.
- **(4) Whether the chat refund path has ever fired.** The one readable window has no refund events, and there is no counter.
- **(4) How much has accrued in `builder:earned:<wallet>`.** It is stored per wallet in KV.
- **(4) Whether a purchase marker ever failed to become permanent and was credited twice.** That would show only as a `[kv:set] purchase:...` runtime log line.
- **(4) Who the relayer 0xdb9b1e94... is, and what selector 0xcef6d209 means.** Blockscout returned internal errors, and no signature database was used.
- **(4) How many of the 29 airdrop claims went to addresses the claimer does not control.** The claim route stores no signature and exposes no addresses.
- **(5) Whether alert-drain, archive-watch and user-tasks ran in the last 7 days.** None writes a public artifact, and logs returned 403.
- **(5) Whether sparkline-refresh, acp-poll and brief-worker ran on every tick.** The artifacts prove individual runs, not continuity.
- **(5) Vercel's own registered-cron list and invocation history.** `get_project` and `get_runtime_logs` returned 403 ('Not authorized ... scope madebyshuns-projects').
- **(5) Why sparkline-refresh stops after ticker 11.** That needs runtime logs. A kill at maxDuration=180 s fits, but is unproven.
- **(5) Whether any DCA schedule with a live USDC allowance exists.** The data is KV-only, and the per-wallet routes need real addresses.
- **(5) Whether any wallet is enrolled in user-tasks or linked to Telegram for alert-drain.** Both are KV-only.
- **(5) Whether Telegram's webhook points at `/api/telegram/webhook`, and whether any update arrived in 7 days.** Checking needs the bot token.
- **(5) Whether a QStash schedule still exists on the Upstash account.** Checking needs a QStash token.
- **(5) Whether anyone else has been calling `B20HUB_BUYBACK.distribute()`.** Only the current balance and the threshold were read.
- **(5) Why arrow #0629 has been stuck pending for 4 days.** `bh:brief:queue` is KV-only.
- **(5) Whether the off-repo Aeon job writes production KV.** The only link is a Discord-webhook secret whose value was not read. Its outputs are discarded to `/dev/null`, and GitHub keeps no response body.
- **(5) Whether any scheduler runs on hosts other than the audit host**, for example the Mac Mini named in the `services/x402-server` README. Only this machine's launchd, crontab and Claude scheduled tasks were checked.
- **(6) The complete list of Vercel production env var names.** The Vercel MCP's `filter_project_envs` returns values (plaintext for plain vars) and cannot be limited to names, so it was not called. The 2026-09-25 CLI pull used instead is provably incomplete and 4 days old.
- **(6) Whether the 41 env vars that are read but not visible are unset in production.** Same cause. Confirming `AEON_WEBHOOK_SECRET` from outside would take a write POST to `/api/webhook/aeon`.
- **(6) Whether the Bankr storefront's LLM key works, and whether Bankr settles a payment when a handler returns 500.** The handler source is known: every handler calls `llm.bankr.bot`, 29 return 500 on failure, and 2 return a 200 with a fixed verdict. The `LLM_KEY` in Bankr's cloud env cannot be read, Bankr's settlement code is not public, and finding out would mean paying. Why `quantum-batch` answers 404 while it is in the manifest is also unknown. On-chain, section 7 shows settlements forwarded to 0xf31f59 through 2026-09-19.
- **(6) Whether any external user installed and ran the npm packages.** Download counts mix mirrors, scanners and CI, and npm has no per-user telemetry.
- **(6) Whether anyone has installed `packages/claude-plugin`.** It is distributed from the GitHub repo, so there are no registry counts.
- **(6) What local `packages/*/dist` contains.** It is gitignored, so local state was measured from HEAD src.
- **(6) Env vars that libraries read implicitly, beyond `@vercel/kv`.** Only `@vercel/kv`'s dist was grepped.
- **(6) Where `@blueagent/x402-guard`'s source lives.** It is in no repo checked (this one, all branches, and `blueagent-x402-services`). Private repos of other owners cannot be searched.
